  - R3-34 · Medium · The chat's advice guard is a short list: "I'd buy it", "put in an offer", "you should make an offer" pass — _In adviceIn, also match BANNED_PHRASES (at least its Advice, Guarantees and Predictions groups) with the briefing validator's phraseRe, so one list governs every written channel._
  - R3-35 · Medium · A price or percentage written in words bypasses the figure guard ("5 pounds", "8 pence", "10 percent") — _Extend FIGURE's suffix group with \s?(pounds?\|pence\|quid\|per\s?cent\|percent) (money / percent kinds) so a worded unit is checked like a symbol._
  - R3-36 · Medium · Full-view text is shown verbatim as it streams, before the figure and advice guard runs — _The allowed set is known before the round (account, question, state.sources, history): check the accumulated text on each delta and send {type:'clear'} at the first disallowed figure or advice phrase, finishing the round silently; or buffer the final (tool_choice none) round until the guard has run._
  - R3-38 · Medium · The chat system prompt tells the model both to round figures and to write them exactly as given — _Make the 'numbers' rule channel-specific (in_app_spoken and phone) or override it in CHANNELS.chat so the chat prompt carries one instruction._
  - R3-33 · Medium · A question the knowledge base does cover is answered "I don't know that one yet" and logged as a gap when the matched entry's show_when is false for this member or needs a value they don't have — _In lookUpKnowledge, when the top entry renders 'hidden' or 'skip', fall through to the next match above answerMin; if none, return outcome 'answered' with the slug and an empty answers list so the caller can say "That doesn't apply to your account" (or at least log the slug as knowledge_ref so the gap job treats it as covered)._
  - R3-48 · Medium · The phone agent's prompt hard-codes a 'why did you call me' answer for deal calls: a second copy of the approved why_called / standout_calls entries — _In callTask's `context "deal"` line delete the clause from 'if they ask why they were called' to 'times a month' and replace it with 'if they ask why they were called, answer from [why_called] and [standout_calls] in the knowledge below' (both are REQUIRED-channel call entries once approved); re-sync the agent. Same for the one-line 'missed_deal' context._
  - R3-43 · Medium · SENSITIVE_TERMS.people lists bare pronouns (he, she, him, her, his, hers), so a fact the chat tool asks for "in the third person" ("He only wants 2-bed flats") is refused as sensitive after the member taps Yes — _Drop the six bare pronouns from SENSITIVE_TERMS.people (keep the relationship nouns), or change the propose_fact description to the subject-less form its own example uses ("Only wants 2-beds") and add a test that a pronoun-led property preference passes checkFact._
  - R3-21 · Medium · PRODUCT_FACTS.reportSections = 10 is the report's maximum, not its count: a standard Full analysis renders 7–8 sections, so the analyser_output answer ("A 10-section report") is wrong on calls and in the chat for most members — _Stop quoting a count: reword analyser_output without {report_sections} (list what every report has), and either delete reportSections from PRODUCT_FACTS or make the placeholder a range resolved from sections.ts (count of the always-present sections to ALL.length) so the figure cannot be wrong for any report. Re-approve the entry._

  - R3-85 · Low · "I don't know in the model's own words" is detected only by one exact phrase; any paraphrased non-answer without figures is charged — _Widen saysDontKnow to a small set of non-answer patterns ("don't know", "can't find", "no information", "not able to") when the answer has no figures and no tool results were used._

  - R3-76 · Low · The agent's scripted line for an unknown question says "I've passed it to the team" but log_question passes nothing to anyone — _Change the scripted line to "I don't know that one yet — it'll help me learn" (or have the prompt call handoff_to_team when the caller wants an answer), and keep the "passed to the team" wording only for handoff_to_team._

The nightly knowledge job and its switch: remember rejected drafts, price every model call, mark failures, do not close a gap for a channel it was never approved for, and keep the quiet maintenance steps running when drafting is off.

- **Files:** `src/lib/knowledge/gap/run.ts`, `src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts`, `src/app/api/internal/si-knowledge/route.ts`, `src/lib/knowledge/config.ts`, `src/lib/knowledge/gap/models-server.ts`, `src/lib/knowledge/seed-server.ts`, `src/lib/knowledge/store-server.ts`, `src/lib/knowledge/gap/plan.ts`
- **Tests to add:** gap-run test: a rejected slug is offered to the grouping model and never re-drafted; a timed-out call still counts towards the cap; a failed insert marks the gap failed; SI_GAP_JOB_ENABLED=false still runs the stale check and retention
- **Smallest fixes:**
  - R3-66 · Medium · Re-drafting a rejected gap is prevented only if Haiku matches the rejected entry's slug; rejected gaps are not offered to the grouping model, so a re-asked question becomes a new open gap and is drafted again — _Offer rejected (and dismissed/failed) gaps to the grouping model as existing gaps too (include them in openGaps with their label; keep the status so applyGroup leaves status as it is), and before inserting a new gap in applyGroup run matchKnowledge-style matching of the new label against the labels of rejected/dismissed gaps and link to one above the answered threshold instead of inserting._
  - R3-37 · Medium · "Ask in the full view" routing is logged as low_confidence, so every one becomes a nightly knowledge gap — _Add a 'routed' outcome (QUESTION_OUTCOMES, the si_conversation_questions check, excluded from GAP_OUTCOMES) and log full_view replies with it; keep low_confidence for guard refusals._
  - R3-57 · Medium · The nightly grouping closes a gap as 'covered' by an approved entry whatever channel the question came from, so a call question answered only by a view/chat entry is never drafted for calls and the phone agent keeps saying it doesn't know — _Give the grouping prompt each entry's channels (`${e.slug} (${e.status}; ${e.channels.join('/')})`) and in applyGroup treat a match as 'covered' only when the entry's channels include every channel in the group's questions (`g.questionIds.map(id => qs.get(id)?.channel)`); otherwise leave the gap 'open' with entry_id set so the draft can propose a call variant. Alternatively run grouping per channel._
  - R3-131 · Low · SI_GAP_JOB_ENABLED=false also switches off the nightly stale check, the agent catch-up sync, the Monday gaps email and question retention, none of which spend anything — _In route.ts, when the job is off run runGapJob with a new flag (e.g. housekeepingOnly: true) that does steps 1–3 and skips the model calls, or move the weekly email and retention into their own step the cron always runs._
  - R3-132 · Low · Two stale comments say the nightly job drafts for up to 240 s; GAP_TIME_BUDGET_MS is 150 s — _Change both comments to read the constant ("GAP_TIME_BUDGET_MS, 150 s")._
  - R3-133 · Low · PRODUCT_FACTS points the management fee at a file that does not exist — _Point the comment at src/lib/analysis.ts (and the management page copy)._
  - R3-143 · Low · A drafted entry's variants are the members' phrasings cut at 300 characters while the entry limit is 200, so a long question produces a draft Zac cannot approve until he trims it by hand — _`variants: samples.map(redactQuestion).filter(...).map((s) => s.slice(0, VARIANT_MAX_CHARS)).slice(0, 5)` (import VARIANT_MAX_CHARS from ../config), or drop samples longer than the limit._
  - R3-144 · Low · A model call that times out (and is retried once by the SDK) counts 0p towards the monthly cap although Anthropic may have billed one or two completed requests — _Set `maxRetries: 0` (the job already retries the next night) and on a timeout add the call's worst case to report.costPence via recordCost(worst) so the cap counts it._
  - R3-145 · Low · A gap whose drafted entry cannot be inserted is bumped to draft_attempts 2 but never marked 'failed', so it sits 'open' on the Gaps page for ever and is silently skipped every night — _In the createEntry failure branch compute `attempts = gap.draft_attempts + 1` and set `status: attempts >= MAX_DRAFT_ATTEMPTS ? 'failed' : 'open'` as the parse branch does._
  - R3-146 · Low · Once Zac retires a seeded entry, any later change to its seed text makes every Seed run report '1 failed … changed since you opened it' for ever — _Select `retired_at` in runKnowledgeSeed's row read, pass it to planSeed as a SeedRowState field and put retired slugs in `skipped` with reason 'retired'._
  - R3-147 · Low · Retiring an entry leaves its gaps 'covered', so the Gaps page and Coverage keep reporting a question as answered by an entry no channel can give any more — _In retireEntry, after the update: `await admin.from('si_knowledge_gaps').update({ status: 'open', match_kind: null, decided_at: null, updated_at: now }).eq('entry_id', o.id).eq('status', 'covered');`_
  - R3-148 · Low · If a Haiku/Sonnet unit_costs row is missing the job prices every call at 0p, so the monthly cap can never trip and the run spends freely — _In runGapJob, after building `price`, check all eight units of MODEL_UNITS resolve to > 0 and otherwise stop with `report.stopped = 'no_price'` (or throw before any model call)._
  - R3-113 · Low · LIVE_CACHE_MS is declared as the store's cache window but nothing reads it: store-server.ts says 'Nothing is cached' — _Delete `LIVE_CACHE_MS` from config.ts._

## 8. Questions before I fix (defaults in bold)

1. **Which findings to fix.** The fix plan in §7 proposes every Critical and High, the Mediums that sit on a new member's first day or on the weekly-active number, and the Lows that ride along in a file a branch already touches. **Default: fix the Critical and High branches first (r3a–r3i), then the Medium branches (r3j–r3m), Lows only where the file is already open.** Tell me any id to drop or any Medium to pull forward.
2. **R2's open findings.** R2's fix branches were never built, and 29 of its 38 ids are still open (§5.4). §7 re-proposes only the ones on a new member's first day (R2-159, R2-160, R2-94, R2-67, R2-1, R2-2, R2-152, R2-122, R2-156) and the message-cap ones that Batch 25 touched (R2-23, R2-24, R2-43, R2-45, R2-46). The rest stay listed, not re-planned. **Default: as proposed.** If you want the full R2 plan rebuilt instead, say so and I will re-cut it in R2's branch shape.
3. **The Batch 23b schema section.** `briefing_day_counts` and `member_briefings` are not on live, so the briefings cron answers 500 twice a day and the briefing is silently absent from every email. The fix is to run `supabase/schema.sql` on live (🗄️, nothing to code), then re-check `?dry=1`. **Default: you run `schema.sql` before any r3 branch merges, and r3c adds the guard so a missing table never 500s the run.** If you would rather switch the briefing off until it is wanted, say so and r3c removes the two cron entries instead.
4. **The spoken answer's price.** The approved `chat_voice` entry tells a member a spoken answer costs "a little on top", and the code charges per character at the `/api/speak` rate, which is more than the typed answer's ceiling. Two honest fixes: (a) price the speak step from a setting and put that figure in the entry as a placeholder, or (b) cap the speak charge at a setting (for example the quick ceiling) and say so. **Default: (a), with the entry re-drafted for your approval, never approved in code.**
5. **Who may be called back while calls are off.** PRs #135 and #138 describe the inbound surface as working while `SI_CALLS_ENABLED` is off, which R2-27 called a finding. If that is intended, the review closes R2-27 as "by design" and r3h only adds the consent check (R2-26/R2-28). **Default: by design; add the consent check.**
6. **The Meta events on a pack purchase.** The starter pack fires a Meta `Purchase` event (Batch 20's design). That is consistent and fires once, so it is not a finding; it is noted because the go/no-go bullet says "events still firing once", which they do. **Default: leave as is.**
7. **`npm audit`.** `next@16.4.0` is a non-major upgrade that closes six high advisories in a runtime package; the rest are dev tooling. **Default: the code-health branch (r3n) bumps `next` and runs the full build and test; the dev-tooling advisories are left (semver-major).** Say if you would rather hold `next` until after the ad week.
8. **Live reads during the fix branches.** The review read live (`select` only). The fix branches will need the same to confirm settings and the 23b run. **Default: yes, `select` only, as now.**
9. **Branch shape.** One small branch per group as in §7, each with its own tests and PR, in the order given, each rebased on main after the one before merges. **Default: as proposed.** If you prefer fewer, larger branches, say which groups to merge.

### D0. What the completeness critic flagged, and what was done about it

After the readers and the first sweep, a critic read every bullet of the brief against the findings and the clean lines below. Three bullets had no evidence either way and went to a gap reader (all three came back built, with the file: the full view's 11 tools against the brief's list, the reworded "I've noticed" prompts with Passes and deal type as signals, and the knowledge table's attributes). The critic also marked these as thin, and they stay thin in this report:

- **R2's fixes "each on main with its test":** §5.4 is the systematic walk (reader HR2, all 38 ids). Tests are named for R2-13, R2-86, R2-87, R2-193, R2-194 and R2-172; the deal-call index (R2-168) has no test of its own.
- **"Not for me" with a 5-second undo:** built as asked. The row offers the undo for 5 seconds (`UNDO_MS` in `src/app/my-deals/_components/SavedForYou.tsx`); the server accepts it for 60 seconds (`UNDO_WINDOW_MS` in `src/lib/standout/saved-server.ts`) so a slow tap is not refused.
- **A new member's first day "with no errors" on a phone:** code walk only (appendix E, walk 1). No device walk-through was possible; it is first on the click-through list in §1.
- **Every cron's `?dry=1`:** the six new routes and the daily digest and picks dry runs were checked; the other routes rest on R1 and R2 under the "only re-check where touched" rule.
- **The 90-day deletion "actually runs":** the code path and the 02:40 schedule were checked; whether the job has run on live was not read (no `anonymised_at` rows were queried). One `select` on live settles it.
- **Prompt injection:** a code walk through the guards and prompts, not a live probe (§8, question 4 in R3's plan; `scripts/chat-eval.mjs` needs an API key this container does not have).
- **The nudge email's 20p:** priced through the shared charge helper from `si_email_pence` (reader B2, appendix E3); the critic wanted it stated in one line, so here it is.
- **Twilio failing mid-call:** covered only by the stale-ringing reconcile and the "unknown" outcome finding (**R3-5**); a member whose call drops hears nothing further and is charged for the seconds the webhook reports.
- **"Anything planned still owed":** no new TODO or "later batch" comment in code; what is owed is the Batch 23b deploy step and R2's 29 open ids (§5.4).
- **`schema.sql` twice:** one mechanical run (appendix A), qualified by the two findings on inline-created check lists and the Batch 22e backfill.
- **The "shown" record:** the reveal's `shown_ids` is only ever written equal to `deal_ids` (R3 finding on R2-92); Today's shown rows and the standout's "seen before" read the same tables (reader F+I, §6).


**Yield of the sweeps, for the record.** Sweep 1 (the 33 files no reader had opened in full, three chunks) reported 9 candidates; after dedupe and verification 9 stand (3 Medium, 6 Low) and 1 High was refuted (appendix F). The gap reader added 2 Lows. Sweep 2 (money and idempotency, 126 core files, eight chunks) reported 47 candidates; 45 stand (1 High, 17 Medium, 27 Low), with one High re-graded Medium by its refuters. Most of sweep 2's findings sit in the briefing runner, the nightly knowledge job and the standout's edge cases, which is where a second pair of eyes was worth having; none changes the go / no-go.

- supabase/schema.sql:7182,7189-7210 + src/lib/knowledge/facts-server.ts:44-56: si_fact_remember takes a per-member advisory xact lock, checks lower(fact) duplicates and the cap before inserting, matching si_member_facts_once_uidx (user_id, lower(fact)); the code passes max = si_facts_max (30 on live).
- supabase/schema.sql:6865 + src/lib/briefing/store.ts:145-170: claimBriefing inserts (user_id, uk_day, status 'generating', action_id) against unique (user_id, uk_day); a 23505 re-reads the row and reclaims only a stale 'generating' row by a conditional update on created_at.

**B1** (24)

- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:235 startHold → startAction with maxBasePence = hold, requireCredit: true, markupOverride = si_chat_markup: the reservation is taken before any model call and refused even with CREDIT_ENFORCE off (action.ts:54-64, http.ts:35-39)
- src/lib/chat/budget.ts:52-58 budgetFor: hold = min(ceiling, spendable − 0.01), null under the floor; spendable comes from credit_available.spendable_base_pence which already divides each grant by its spend rate and subtracts open reservations (schema.sql:715-739), so the hold can never exceed what the buckets cover
- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:338-348 meterRounds: one meter() per token type per round at the right rows (quick → haiku45_*, full → sonnet55_*; cache reads at *_cache_read_token, cache writes at *_cache_write_token); priced once with ctx.markupOverride = 5 (chargeLines markup 1 only yields quantities); unit rows' own markup is 5 so no double application; the grant spend rate is applied once inside credit_debit
- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:277-303 settle: the pending → status update with .eq('status','pending') is the single decision; only the winner meters; charge only when status 'answered' and not admin; chargeOnce pattern matches briefing/runner.ts:296-303 (meter with skipPreflight under the action ctx, then actionSpend)
- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:309-311 cap scaling: when costOf(rounds) > capPence the markup is scaled so the total is cap − 0.01p; combined with roundMaxTokens' ROUNDING_MARGIN the charge cannot pass the hold (ceiling or balance) even on a prompt-cache miss
- src/lib/chat/budget.ts:107-126 roundMaxTokens prices new prompt tokens at max(input, cache_write) and cached at cache_read, returns 0 under MIN_ROUND_OUTPUT_TOKENS or with no output price; unitsPriced refuses to ask when any of the four rows is missing (quick-server.ts:50, full-server.ts:83)
- src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:165-263 the look-up loop re-checks live.gone(), the 52 s hard clock, the 35 s look-up deadline, fullMaxToolRounds (4) and the budget every round; a round that cannot afford 120 output tokens ends as out_of_room, uncharged
- src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:184-195 Sonnet 5.5 request shape is valid per the claude-api skill: thinking {type:'between_tools'} with output_config.effort 'low', tool_choice auto/none (never any/tool), top-level cache_control plus a system breakpoint; cached tokens are read from usage.cache_read_input_tokens via usageOf (budget.ts:32-35)
- src/lib/credit/costs.ts:208-217 seed prices match the model table in the claude-api skill (Sonnet 5.5 $2/$10, cache read $0.20, write $2.50; Haiku 4.5 $1/$5, read $0.10, write $1.25) and facts.md confirms the eight rows exist on live
- src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:95-99 max_tokens is sized from the hold for the real prompt (~1,500 tokens): a 2p floor still allows the full 200-token cap; typical quick answer ≈ 0.77p base so the 1p hint is honest
- src/lib/chat/full-server.ts measured with the seed prices: a one-round answer costs 1.0p (cache hit) / 3.4p (miss), three rounds with a first-round miss 9.1p, three rounds with no cache 12.3p — the 8p hint is between the one- and three-round cases, and the admin page tells Zac to set it from the median (admin/intelligence/chat/page.tsx:122)
- src/lib/chat/*.ts: no literal pence or price in code (grep); hints, floors, ceilings, markup, min seconds and the daily cap are billing_settings rows parsed with bounds and defaults (settings.ts:203-308); the components take them from chatUi() (turns-server.ts:65-69) and the knowledge placeholders read the same rows (placeholders.ts:300-305)
- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:178-199 preflight: existing client id → stored result; stale pending (>2 min) failed; si_chat_min_seconds enforced against the member's last turn with TOO_FAST_LINE; si_chat_max_uncharged_per_day counts no_answer plus failed-with-rounds per UK day with UNCHARGED_CAP_LINE, admins exempt, answered questions never capped
- supabase/schema.sql:7644-7645 chat_turns_client_uidx (user_id, client_turn_id) and chat_turns_pending_uidx (user_id) where pending back the retry and one-in-flight guards; insertTurn maps 23505 to existing/busy (turns-server.ts:211-215)
- src/app/api/chat/quick/route.ts and full/route.ts: the member is always the session user; clientTurnId must be a uuid; same-origin JSON check; work continues under after() so settle and hold.finish() run after a disconnect; vercel.json opts both (and listen) into supportsCancellation so request.signal fires
- src/components/intelligence/chat/QuickChat.tsx:143-147 and FullChat.tsx:125-129: a retry that comes back did_not_finish is re-sent under a NEW id (the first was never charged); busy/too_fast keep the same id for Try again; a client abort is only on unmount
- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:99-104 + credit/action.ts:42-47: a team member's question reserves and debits the owner's credit (payer_id on chat_turns, member_id on each debit); paused seats refused before any hold; no top-up buttons for members (actions.ts:97-100)
- src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:187-192 / full-server.ts:336: activity is si_chat_quick (record only) and si_chat_full (qualifying) per activity/kinds.ts:227-228, logged once per settled question, after the answer
- src/lib/credit/usage-label.ts:278-279 si_chat_quick/si_chat_full → 'Stayful Intelligence question'; history.ts groups every debit of the turn's action id into one row; admin Chat tab's cost/revenue come from chat_turns.raw_pence/charged_base_pence/charged_face_pence which settle() fills from costOf and actionSpend(turn.id) over the same credit_transactions rows (turns-server.ts:293,315-316; admin-server.ts:199)
- src/app/api/chat/listen/route.ts:83 failed: (r) => !r.ok || !r.text → a recording ElevenLabs cannot read or that yields no text is logged at £0 and never charged; the route requires sign-in, same origin, multipart, size caps and the chat+voice switches
- src/lib/chat/log-server.ts:249-257 markUnhappy and 267-279 answerFact: no money moves; the fact claim is single-use (is('fact_proposal->>answered', null))
- src/lib/chat/tools-server.ts: no look-up meters or charges anything (reads only); the one external wait is getAreaCardsWithin(1.5 s)
- supabase/schema.sql:7607-7611 R2-87 (source 'chat') is on main and live per facts.md; log-server.ts:76 records every chat question with source 'chat'
- src/lib/credit/action.ts, meter.ts, ledger.ts, pricing.ts: unchanged since R2 (git diff 5d0ac80 HEAD -- src/lib/credit shows only after-debit, costs, low-credit-server, unit-costs, usage-label); the chat relies on the reviewed reserve → debit(reservation) → release path

**C2** (47)

- src/app/api/chat/quick/route.ts and full/route.ts: the member is supabase.auth.getUser() only; the body carries clientTurnId/question/conversationId and nothing else is read
- src/lib/chat/tools.ts: no tool schema has a user/member/account/email/owner field (tools.test.ts enforces it); strict schemas, additionalProperties false; parseToolInput re-checks uuid/stage/date
- src/lib/chat/tools-server.ts: chatTools is built per question from the session member; every query filters by member.userId/payerId; deal_facts/todays_picks go through dealCardsByIds(ids, visibility) so a free member inside the 48h delay gets 'available:false' / nothing
- src/lib/chat/tools-server.ts dealFacts: no address, full postcode or listing link field (town + outcode, price line, profit, numbers); my_profile_summary returns areas/where only
- src/lib/chat/tools-server.ts deal_facts: the report is read through the member's own supabase client (RLS) and only when cardStatesFor gives a reportId for member/payer
- src/lib/chat/tools-server.ts offer_action: buildButton requires the deal id to be in state.deals/showMe from this answer's look-ups; deep_search only while deepQuoteFor offers it and no search runs
- src/lib/chat/actions.ts: open_deal/full_analysis/show_me targets must be UUIDs the answer showed; use_this needs isWhatIfKey and an offered key; quick surface limited to QUICK_ACTIONS; at most 3 buttons; no model-supplied href ever reaches the page
- src/components/intelligence/chat/ChatAnswer.tsx: buttons are <Link href> or applyWhatIfAction on member onClick; no auto-run on render
- src/components/intelligence/what-if-actions.ts: applyWhatIfAction re-derives the change from intelligenceMember(user,'header') and whatIfChanges, so a forged key only makes an offered change
- src/lib/chat/prompts.ts: system prompts are fixed text with no member data; questionBlock wraps the question in <question> and strips the closing tag; history goes back as plain text (no thinking blocks)
- src/lib/chat/full-server.ts: every tool_use input goes through parseToolInput; unknown names get an is_error tool_result; thinking is never streamed; answer checked (figures, advice, don't-know) before logExchange/settle; buttons and factProposal only on replyState 'answer'
- src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts: slug must be one of the matched entries; figure and advice guards run before the answer is shown; needs_full_view returns the fixed FULL_VIEW_LINE with the open_full_view button only
- src/lib/chat/knowledge-server.ts: liveEntries('chat') → si_knowledge_live; only an 'answered' match at readKnowledgeSettings thresholds is rendered (renderEntry) for this member
- src/lib/knowledge/store-server.ts: member reads (liveEntries/renderLive/answerBySlug) select from si_knowledge_live only; allEntries/entryById (si_knowledge) are used only by admin pages, the seed, the gap job and coverage/weekly counts
- supabase/schema.sql si_knowledge_live (line 6996): filters answer/question/category not null, stale_reason is null, retired_at is null; no draft column; revoked from anon/authenticated
- supabase/schema.sql si_knowledge_approve: version + draft hash guard; mode 'live' only clears stale_reason on a stale row; nothing in schema.sql inserts or approves a knowledge row
- src/lib/knowledge/seed-server.ts + seed.ts planSeed: inserts and proposes drafts only; never approves; never replaces a pending non-seed draft
- src/lib/knowledge/render.ts checkContent: approval refuses typed digits/£/%/links/emails (figures.ts), unknown or dead placeholders, member values/sections/show_when on call entries
- src/lib/knowledge/placeholders.ts: every global figure resolves from billing_settings/billing_plans/unit_costs with no defaults; call_minute_cost uses the si:call_minute unit row × markup (the charge's own); standout_call_floor uses callFloorPence
- src/lib/knowledge/agent.ts: agentKnowledge takes only entries with channel 'call', skips member values, sections, show_when and figures that don't resolve now; KNOWLEDGE_VARIABLES fixed in code; REQUIRED_CALL_SLUGS guard refuses the cut-over sync, never substitutes a draft
- src/lib/knowledge/agent-server.ts syncAgentKnowledge: after cut-over syncs whatever is live (retired/stale entries leave the agent); afterKnowledgeChange runs under after() on approve/retire/stale
- src/lib/voice/agent/sync-server.ts line 137: a prompt that uses a {{variable}} no call sends is refused; unknownCallerDefaults cover every variable with no secret__ and no member value
- src/lib/voice/agent/variables.ts callVariables: every k_* variable is sent on every call, fallback 'shown in the app'; deal_headline/deal_short from standout/copy.ts (town, beds, rounded range; never an address, postcode, price or link)
- src/lib/voice/agent/prompt.ts: the agent's knowledge section is only the synced live text; tools described as instructions; {{k_standout_calls_per_month}} is a sent variable
- src/lib/voice/tools-server.ts: every tool resolves the live call by ElevenLabs-injected ids (liveCall), rate-limited per call; lookup_caller ignores the number argument; send_template_text only to the number on file and refuses a mismatch with the number on the call; answers carry no balance/address/deal figure
- src/lib/voice/agent/tools.ts: injected ids carry only dynamic_variable; the tool secret header comes from secret__tool_token; no tool takes a member or user id
- src/lib/voice/sms-replies-server.ts: fixed reply strings, no model, no knowledge read; members' words only forwarded to the admin address
- src/lib/intelligence/view-server.ts: chips from renderLive('view', memberValuesFrom(facts)); the Part F line reaches the no_match entry only via member values; whatIfViewFor is the same function the chat's what_if_suggestions calls
- src/lib/intelligence/answers.ts: chipsFor only offers slugs with a rendered live entry; buttons fixed in code
- src/lib/knowledge/gap/prompts.ts: questions redacted (email, link, postcode, long numbers) before any model; grouping/drafting have no tools; parseGrouping/parseDraft drop unknown keys and slugs; DRAFT_INSTRUCTIONS forbid digits/£/%, sections and advice
- src/lib/knowledge/gap/run.ts: drafts created with source 'gap' as pending drafts; gaps matched to a rejected entry get status 'rejected'; draftCandidates skips entries with entry_id or status != open; MAX_DRAFT_ATTEMPTS 2
- src/lib/knowledge/gap/models-server.ts: every model call metered with userId null (house spend) under GAP_ACTION
- src/lib/knowledge/weekly-server.ts + weekly.ts: Monday email to feedbackSettings().adminEmail; labels redacted and escaped; never in the subject
- src/lib/knowledge/facts-server.ts + facts-rules.ts: rememberFact refuses not_confirmed/empty/too_long/sensitive/personal_details; reads/deletes filtered by user_id
- src/app/api/chat/facts/route.ts: member from session; answerFact only the proposal on that member's own turn; claimed once
- src/app/api/chat/turns/[id]/unhappy/route.ts: markUnhappy filtered by user_id and status 'answered'
- src/app/api/chat/history/route.ts: GET/DELETE scoped to user.id; delete refused while a question is pending
- src/app/api/chat/listen/route.ts: transcript is returned to the page and sent as an ordinary question (every chat guard applies); chat and voice switches checked
- src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts chatOn(): reads both SI_CHAT_ENABLED and billing_settings.si_chat_enabled (live true/true per facts.md)
- src/lib/chat/settings.ts + facts.md: ceilings/floors/hints/markup/history/idle/min seconds/max uncharged all read from si_chat_* rows; live values match DEFAULT_CHAT_SETTINGS
- src/lib/chat/admin-server.ts: admin panel reads counts and question text only (no member name), rendered by React
- src/components/intelligence/chat/FullChat.tsx and QuickChat.tsx: chip answers are server-rendered approved text; the ?ask= question is only placed in the box, never auto-submitted; spoken answers use the final checked reply.text
- src/components/intelligence/chat/voice.ts: nothing is sent or charged on a silent auto-listen; the mic is closed while speaking
- scripts/chat-eval.mjs: read-only real-API probe using the app's prompt and guards; writes nothing
- src/lib/persona/stayful-intelligence.ts chat rules: chat_only_given, chat_no_advice, chat_buttons, chat_privacy, chat_instructions present; PERSONA_VERSION bumped to si-voice-v3
- src/lib/knowledge/config.ts PRODUCT_FACTS: 10 report pages (src/lib/pdf/report has 10 page components), 12 months, 15% (src/lib/analysis.ts:18), six properties (methodology page), 2025 — values match the code

**D** (37)

- vercel.json: /api/internal/standout (35 * * * *, 58 6 * * *), si-knowledge (50 2), si-chat (40 2), si-calls (*/5), briefings (40 6, 55 6) are all listed and every route file exists; maxDuration set in each route, not vercel.json (standout 120, si-calls 120, si-knowledge 300, si-chat 60, briefings 60).
- src/lib/internal-auth.ts + all five routes: internalSecretsConfigured() → 404 and authoriseInternal() → 401 run before any read or write; constant-time compare; N8N_SHARED_SECRET not accepted on these routes.
- src/app/api/internal/standout/route.ts + src/lib/standout/run.ts: ?dry=1 → apply=false gates every write (writeMemberDecisions/writeDealDecisions/saveStandout behind `if (!c.apply) return`, recheckDeal and requestCheck behind c.apply, processNotifications apply=false returns would_* strings, processNudges apply=false, recordRun and purgeOldDecisions behind apply). A dry run writes nothing, including no standout_runs row.
- src/lib/standout/run.ts: STANDOUT_ENABLED=false with a real cron → apply=false, so the pass only judges; `?only=` never moves the watermarks (`!o.onlyUserId` in recordRun).
- src/lib/standout/run.ts + server.ts: safe to run twice — standout_decisions_deal_uidx (user_id, deal_id) with upsert ignoreDuplicates, member rows via standout_decisions_member_uidx (23505 ignored), saveStandout claims saved_at from null, a Keep 23505 → 'answered' and the decision reverts; a second concurrent pass gets no decision id and saves nothing.
- src/lib/standout/notify-server.ts: claimNotify (`.is('notify', null)`) guards call/email/none/below_floor so two passes cannot queue two calls or send twice; a claim with no call_id is re-planned only after STUCK_CLAIM_MS (10 min) via syncPass; a decision whose text step died after claimNotify is carried by 'Saved for you' (notify='email', emailed_at null), never re-texted.
- src/lib/standout/notify-server.ts belowFloor + saved-server.ts savedForYouFor: a sent below-floor email stamps emailed_at, and savedForYouFor filters `.is('emailed_at', null)`, so the standout email and the daily 'Saved for you' never both describe the same deal (except a crash between send and the stamp).
- src/lib/standout/notify.ts planMember: 3rd standout in a UK month → placedThisMonth ≥ callsPerMonth → action 'email' status monthly_limit → notify 'email' → listed under 'Saved for you' (saved-server.ts) if the deal is still live and the type still shown.
- src/lib/standout/calls-server.ts dealCallsThisMonth: counted per UK calendar month (londonMonthStart, Europe/London via Intl), placed rows only (placed_at not null); blocked rows never count; checked at plan time (notify-server.ts:120) and again at dial time (calls-server.ts:226-228). Live standout_calls_per_month=2 matches DEFAULT_STANDOUT.
- src/lib/voice/queue-server.ts + store-server.ts dayFacts: the one-outbound-call-a-day rule spans intro, low_credit and deal — placedToday counts every outbound row with uk_day = today; the claim `update … set status='ringing', uk_day` is refused by si_calls_log_one_a_day_uidx (23505 → blocked daily_limit). Deal calls placed from the standout pass go through the same placeCall.
- src/lib/voice/eligibility.ts + hours.ts: weekdays 09:00–19:00 from si_outbound_weekdays [1..5], start 9, end 19 (live values match); londonParts/ukWeekday use Intl Europe/London so DST is handled; nextOpening/nextDayOpening step whole UTC hours (UK offsets are whole hours).
- src/lib/voice/eligibility.ts:69 + member-server.ts:55: not_owner for team members (teamOf role) at queue and dial; run.ts loadMembers also skips team members with reason team_member.
- src/lib/standout/run.ts:313-318 and calls-server.ts:184-187: the PRIMARY profile is primaryOf(allProfilesFor) — the same rule as profiles/server.ts primaryProfileFor (earliest live) — never activeProfileFor; the Keep is written with profile_id = primary and /my-deals shows every profile's deals unless ?profile= is given, so the save is visible whichever profile is active.
- src/lib/standout/run.ts:331 and calls-server.ts:178: deal types come from Batch 17's dealTypesFor (src/lib/profile/deal-types.ts), the same helper profile/state.ts uses for Today; no second list.
- src/lib/standout/notify-server.ts:115 + queue-server.ts:91 + calls-server.ts:212 + run.ts(voice):107: STANDOUT_CALLS_ENABLED and SI_CALLS_ENABLED are checked at plan time (switchedOn), SI_CALLS_ENABLED at enqueue and in the si-calls cron, STANDOUT_CALLS_ENABLED again in dealCallStillWanted at dial; the member's si_calls switch (memberFacts.callsOn) is read at enqueue and again in placeCall.
- src/lib/standout/calls-server.ts dealCallStillWanted: just before dialling re-reads the decision (not opened/moved/Not for me), deal_reactions.saved_by (still the SI's Keep), the primary profile's chosen types (stillShownFor → shownVerdict), the month count, the balance floor, status live, and liveConfirmed within standout_live_confirm_hours else recheckDeal; a read failure → wait (deferred an hour), never a call on a guess.
- src/lib/standout/recheck.ts: the dial-time and pass-time rechecks run fetchDealPage under runMetered({ userId: null, action: 'standout_verify' }) — house spend, never a member's; pass rechecks capped at standout_rechecks_per_run (3) and 60 s.
- src/lib/standout/run.ts:423-453 + rules.ts savesLeftToday: two standouts in a day → bestFirst (match, then profit) wins, maxPerDay=1 (live) saves one; notify.ts planMember then gives the rest 'one_call' and rings about the best only.
- src/lib/standout/notify.ts retarget + queue-server.ts:223: a better standout retargets the member's queued deal call (trigger_ref changed only while status='queued'); placeCall claims only with `.eq('trigger_ref', call.trigger_ref)` so a call read before a retarget is 'gone' and picked up next pass, never rung about the wrong deal.
- src/lib/standout/texts-server.ts sendMemberText + sms/choose.ts contactCanReceive: standout/nudge texts go only to a verified UK mobile with sms_contacts.enabled and no stopped_at, re-read just before sending; recorded in sms_messages with kind standout|nudge (schema.sql:2701 check widened); Twilio 'opted_out' → stopNumber.
- src/lib/standout/notify.ts:120 + nudge-server.ts:147: below-floor texts and nudge texts wait for inSendingWindow (08:00–20:00 UK); deal_link texts during a call and missed-call fallbacks are inside 09:00–19:00 (+30 min reconcile) by construction.
- src/lib/standout/nudge-server.ts: the nudge is claimed once per landing (credit_nudges unique (user_id, landing_id), 23505 → 'standing'), stamps last_low_balance_email_at so Batch 20's notice is skipped that cycle; sendNudge re-checks auto top-up and balance; text and email each guarded by si_call_charges keys nudge:<id>:text|email (claimCharge is an insert on a unique key) so a second pass cannot charge or send twice; admins never nudged; nothing claimed while STANDOUT_ENABLED is off.
- src/lib/credit/low-credit-server.ts:126-128 + after-debit.ts:149: the nudge is asked wherever the £5 notice is decided (picks run, digest, afterDebit), so the £5 notice and the nudge are never both sent on the same day for the same decision (the same-landing-later case is the finding above).
- src/lib/standout/notify-server.ts syncPass + notify.ts syncFromCall: a deal call's blocked reason in NOTHING_MORE (deal_gone, deal_acted, type_not_chosen, profile_changed) ends it; other blocks/failed → notify null → re-planned, where enqueueCall's 'exists' (si_calls_log_deal_uidx) turns it into 'Saved for you' — a deal is never rung about twice (R2-168 fixed) and a missed call's fallback text/email stamps emailed_at.
- src/lib/voice/fallback-server.ts: handles call_type 'deal' with the deal's own wording and the /si/deal/<token> link; once per call via fallback_sent_at; charges guarded (R2-86 fixed: context stored on the row at enqueue and read back in placeCall via isCallContext).
- src/lib/standout/copy.ts + voice/templates.ts: deal headline/short use town or postcode-area NAME, bedrooms, kind and rounded profit only; texts and emails link to /si/deal/<token> or /my-deals, never the listing; src/app/si/deal/[token]/route.ts exists.
- The 06:58 standout pass vs the 07:00 picks pass: a save at 06:58 gets notify='email' in the same pass (members who cannot be called) and so is in that morning's 'Saved for you'; a callable member's save is notify='call' (queued for 09:00) and stays out of the email; markSavedEmailed sets emailed_at after the send, so a slow 06:58 pass only delays a line to the next day, never duplicates it.
- src/lib/listing/picks-run.ts:1239,1653 and notify/digest-run.ts:292,428: one savedForYouFor read each and markSavedEmailed only after the email is built/sent; message.ts:486 drops a saved deal already told elsewhere in the same email.
- src/app/api/internal/si-knowledge/route.ts + knowledge/gap/run.ts: ?dry=1 claims no si_gap_claim row, writes only its own si_gap_runs 'dry' cost row (so the cap counts it), previews the Monday email without claiming, retention rpc with apply:false; &estimate=1 makes no model call and writes nothing; once a UK day via si_gap_claim(run_key = day); time budget GAP_TIME_BUDGET_MS 150 s + model timeout 60 s (maxRetries 1) fits maxDuration 300; monthly cap re-read before every call, fail-closed when unreadable; every model call metered with userId null (house).
- src/lib/knowledge/weekly-server.ts: Monday email once a week via si_gap_claim('weekly', week) and Resend key si-gaps:<week>; 409 treated as sent; dry sends and claims nothing; to feedback_admin_email, outside the member cap.
- src/app/api/internal/si-chat/route.ts + chat/log-server.ts anonymiseChat: ?dry=1 counts only; apply updates are idempotent (user_id → null) so twice is safe; 45 s budget inside maxDuration 60; days from si_transcript_retention_days (live 90).
- src/app/api/internal/si-calls/route.ts + voice/run.ts: ?dry=1 queues no intro, placeCall apply:false writes nothing (dropOrBlock/updateCall gated), reconcile skipped, retention counted only; twice-safe via the queued→ringing claim, si_calls_log_intro_uidx and claimEvent('cron', retention:<day>); runRequestedPhoneCheck runs on dry too but only writes billing_settings.si_phone_check when si_phone_check_request is newer.
- src/lib/voice/agent/phone-check-server.ts runRequestedPhoneCheck/runPhoneCheck: read-only towards Twilio and ElevenLabs (GETs: phone number, agent, conversations, settings, Notifications, IncomingPhoneNumbers); writes only billing_settings.si_phone_check; si_phone_check_request has no writer anywhere in src (grep), so it can only be set by Zac with the service role — no member-reachable trigger; callers shown as last 3 digits (phone-check.ts).
- src/app/api/internal/briefings/route.ts + briefing/runner.ts: ?dry=1 stores and charges nothing (briefing_day_counts upsert, claimBriefing and finishBriefing gated by !dry); once per member per UK day via member_briefings unique (user_id, uk_day) with a 5-minute reclaim; TIME_BUDGET 35 s + 15 s writer inside maxDuration 60; BRIEFINGS_PASS_ENABLED=false honoured except for dry runs.
- src/lib/standout/settings.ts: every Batch 25 number is a billing_settings row parsed with bounds; the live values in facts.md (min_match 90, min_checked 5, profit_over_min 25, live_confirm 6, calls_per_month 2, call_min_balance 100, max_per_day 1, keep_days 90, rechecks 3, beat_best 30, slower_spender 8/21) equal DEFAULT_STANDOUT; the call floor is max(100p, ceil(65p)+2×22p = 109p) so live members need £1.09.
- src/lib/standout/run.ts:227: the not-standout purge runs once a day at the pass whose London hour is 3 (hourly schedule guarantees one), only when applied and not forced.
- Unit tests run: src/lib/standout/notify.test.ts (12 pass) and src/lib/standout/nudge.test.ts (5 pass).

- src/lib/credit/low-credit-server.ts: lowCreditNoticesFor only claims the nudge when the run is real (apply: !preview), so the daily dry runs write nothing

- src/lib/chat/budget.ts: budgetFor is null below the floor or at £0; roundMaxTokens prices the cached prefix at the read rate and keeps ROUNDING_MARGIN; affordsLookUp stops a look-up that would leave no room for an answer
- src/lib/chat/guard.ts: checkFigures refuses money/percent figures not in the account block, tool results, approved answers, history or the question; adviceIn blocks the recommendation phrases; saysDontKnow makes any 'I don't know that one yet' uncharged

- src/lib/chat/full-server.ts: did_not_finish/failed/out_of_room all settle as 'failed' (house spend) and hold.finish() runs in finally; recordActivity('si_chat_full') only after a delivered reply
- src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts: a quick answer is checked for figures, advice and an unknown slug before it is charged; needs_full_view and unknown are no_answer (house spend); logActivity si_chat_quick record only
- src/lib/chat/log-server.ts: replyForStored never returns a free copy of an undelivered answer; answerFact claims the proposal once (is('fact_proposal->>answered', null)) and saves only on yes through rememberFact
- src/lib/chat/knowledge-server.ts: liveEntries('chat') reads si_knowledge_live only, matched at si_kb_answer_min_confidence/low_confidence_min (live 0.55/0.3); a stale or skipped render gives no answer
- src/app/api/chat/listen/route.ts: STT is charged under the 'speak' action per second, not charged when the service fails or hears nothing; off unless si_chat_voice_enabled and ELEVENLABS_API_KEY
- src/app/api/internal/si-chat/route.ts: ?dry=1 only counts; authoriseInternal; anonymiseChat removes user_id/payer_id and every link back to the question text after 90 days
- src/components/intelligence/chat/send.ts and FullChat.tsx: a retry/reconnect reuses clientTurnId; a fresh id is used only after a confirmed did_not_finish (uncharged)
- src/components/intelligence/AnswerChips.tsx and FullChat.tapChip: Batch 22's chips stay free and log si_view (record only) — they never count towards weekly active
- src/lib/activity/kinds.ts: si_auto_saved, si_deal_call, si_deal_call_missed, si_nudge_sent, si_chat_quick, si_fact_* are record only; si_chat_full, pass (Not for me), deal_open, stage_move, tailoring_prompt are qualifying — matches the brief's E list

- src/app/admin/standout/actions.ts: settings are parsed with parseStandout's bounds before updateBillingSetting; out-of-range values are refused, not saved

| Quick: refusal / max_tokens / unparsable JSON / unknown slug / figure or advice guard fail | house | src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:142-172 | all map to state 'unknown' with DONT_KNOW_LINE; never charged |
| Quick: model call throws or times out (20 s, maxRetries 0) | house | src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:127-134 | logFailedCall (provider_calls ok:false, quantity 0) + settle failed; FAILED_LINE / DID_NOT_FINISH_LINE |
| Quick: page gone before the model call | free | src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:102-105 | no model call; settle failed with rounds [] (not counted by the cap); hold released in finally |
| Quick: page gone during/after the model call | house | src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:121,137-140 | SDK aborted via request.signal (APIUserAbortError) or gone() after the reply; tokens so far metered as house |
| Quick: balance under si_chat_quick_floor_pence (2p) or £0 | free | src/lib/chat/budget.ts:52-58, quick-server.ts:54-55 | budgetFor null → TOP_UP_LINE + Top up button before any row or hold; composer hidden client-side (QuickChat.tsx:102) |
| Quick: hold refused by credit_reserve (race) | free | src/lib/chat/quick-server.ts:72-78 | requireCredit:true → InsufficientCreditError thrown even with CREDIT_ENFORCE off; settle failed, top_up |
| Full: answered (incl. capped at max_tokens, CAPPED_LINE appended) | charged once | src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:301-322 | every round's tokens (cache read/write at their own rows) metered; if cost > hold, markup scaled to hold − 0.01p (turns-server.ts:309-311) |
| Full: don't-know / refusal / empty / figure or advice guard fail | house | src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:272-287 | no_answer; page text replaced with DONT_KNOW_LINE |
| Full: out of room (room 0, look-up asked with none allowed, cut mid tool_use, past the 52 s clock) | house | src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:170,174,223,261 | status failed with rounds>0 → counted by the uncharged cap; OUT_OF_ROOM_LINE |
| Full: stream aborted / page gone / round timeout | house | src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:210-218 | usage so far pushed (output estimated from streamed text) or logFailedCall when none; settle failed |
| Full: balance under si_chat_full_floor_pence (10p) | free | src/lib/chat/full-server.ts:87-88 | plain JSON top_up reply before any row; but 10p–~11.5p can never look up (finding) |
| Retry / reconnect with the same client turn id | free | src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:178-181, log-server.ts:94-113 | preflight 'existing' → replyForStored: answered → stored answer + stored charge label; failed → did_not_finish; pending → busy. Never a second model call |
| Double send / second tab | free | supabase/schema.sql:7645, turns-server.ts:204-216 | chat_turns_pending_uidx (one pending per user) → 23505 → 'busy'; client also gates on busy |
| Tap-to-ask chips (Batch 22 answers) | free | src/components/intelligence/chat/FullChat.tsx:163-166 | tapChip renders the pre-rendered answer; only recordSiViewAction (activity), no chat route |
| QUICK_SUGGESTIONS / FULL_SUGGESTIONS buttons | charged once | src/components/intelligence/chat/QuickChat.tsx:188, FullChat.tsx:258 | ordinary questions via submit(); the full-view ones sit under 'The chips are free.' (finding) |
| Listen (/api/chat/listen) | charged once | src/app/api/chat/listen/route.ts:80-104 | action 'speak' (AI voice), elevenlabs:stt_second × declared seconds (1–30; ≤0.89p); failed/empty transcript → logged at £0; no requireCredit (finding) |
| Speak (/api/speak) for a spoken question's answer | charged once | src/app/api/speak/route.ts:171-193 | elevenlabs:character × text.length (0.0869p/char ≈ 29–42p); failed upstream free; also spoken for free outcomes (finding); no requireCredit (finding) |
| Admin asking | free | src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:305-306 | budget = ceiling, no reservation, charge=false → house ctx with admin:true (bypass logged) |
| Team member asking | charged once | src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:99-104, credit/action.ts:42-47 | payerFor → owner's credit reserved and debited (member_id on each debit); paused seat → SEAT_PAUSED_LINE; TEAM_TOP_UP_LINE instead of Top up |
| 'Not helpful' / 'Want me to remember that?' | free | src/lib/chat/log-server.ts:249-279 | no refund, no charge; fact claim guarded against double tap |
| Uncharged-cap (30/day) and 3-second gap refusals | free | src/lib/chat/turns-server.ts:186-197 | UNCHARGED_CAP_LINE / TOO_FAST_LINE before any row or model call |

#### E3. Money: every charge path of standouts, calls, texts, the nudge and the knowledge AI (reader B2)

| Path | Charged once / free / house | Where | Mechanism |
|---|---|---|---|
| Open an auto-saved deal (Quick look) | free — that deal, that account only | src/lib/marketplace/open.ts:97-98 | isFreeStandoutOpen(payer, deal) needs standout_decisions saved_at set and not_for_me_at null for this user_id+deal_id; price 0, no deal_opens debit (line 195 `if (pence > 0)`); the ladder is untouched for every other deal. A teammate opening it is the owner's account (openDeal userId = payer), so also free. 'Not for me' makes it paid; undo within UNDO_WINDOW_MS (saved-server.ts:152-161) makes it free again. Same deal auto-saved for another member is a separate (user_id, deal_id) row. |
| Deal call: answered minutes | charged once | src/lib/voice/webhook-server.ts:129-131 | post_call_transcription → claimEvent(si_webhook_events) → finishCall → chargeCallMinutes guard `call:<id>:minutes` (charge-server.ts:151); price from the si:call_minute unit row (13p × markup 5 = 65p; placeholders.ts:177-181 render the same sum). settle() caps at callablePence (R2-13) via capToBalance → debitFace: below the charge it is CAPPED, never overdrafts; at £0 charged 0 (house). |
| Deal call: missed / voicemail / failed / nobody spoke | free | src/lib/voice/webhook-server.ts:98-100 | seconds = 0 unless status 'answered' (member spoke and durationSecs > 0); no chargeCallMinutes. |
| Missed deal call: fallback text | charged once (22p, si_text_pence) | src/lib/voice/fallback-server.ts:49-58 | guard `call:<id>:text:deal_link`; fallback_sent_at claim once per call (lines 33-40); deal texts go through sendMemberText (texts switch + STOP + sms_messages). | Missed deal call: fallback email | charged once (20p, si_email_pence) | src/lib/voice/fallback-server.ts:66-77 | guard `call:<id>:email:fallback`; releaseCharge when Resend refuses. |
| deal_link text sent by the agent during a deal call | charged once (22p) | src/lib/voice/tools-server.ts:150-170 | guard `call:<id>:text:deal_link` + per-call slot rows; honours texts switch via sendMemberText. |
| Below the call floor: text | charged once (22p) only if texts on and balance ≥ text+email | src/lib/standout/notify-server.ts:275-286 | floor = max(standout_call_min_balance_pence 100, ceil(perMin 65) + si_texts_per_call_max 2 × si_text_pence 22) = 109p (notify.ts:135-137; README's 'the higher of'); belowFloorChannels (notify.ts:128-132) decides; guard `standout:<decision>:text`; planned only inside 08:00–20:00 UK. |
| Below the call floor: email | charged once (20p) when the balance covers it, else free | src/lib/standout/notify-server.ts:287-303 | chargeEmail branch: guard `standout:<decision>:email` + settleEmail; else sendSiEmail free with idempotency key si-standout:<decision>. Odd corner: texts on + balance 20–41p → free email, texts off + 20p → charged (see notes). |

| Slower-spender nudge: text | charged once (22p) if texts on | src/lib/standout/nudge-server.ts:184-194 | guard `nudge:<id>:text`; claim once per landing (credit_nudges unique (user_id, landing_id)); skipped when auto top-up is on at claim (nudge.ts:62) and again at send (nudge-server.ts:171). Sent only in the 08:00–20:00 window. |
| Slower-spender nudge: email | charged once (20p) if credit alerts on | src/lib/standout/nudge-server.ts:195-205 | guard `nudge:<id>:email`; idempotency si-nudge:<id>. |
| Standout live recheck (page fetch) and open-verify fetch | house | src/lib/standout/recheck.ts:28 | runMetered({ userId: null, action: 'standout_verify' }); open.ts:119 the same for deal_open_verify. |
